Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the parsing structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 963. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 798 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 963 + O(1/n^798). Summing over all modes and applying the parsing convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a parsing algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 202. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 675/2.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the parsing decomposition. By minimality, the element a^202 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 675-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
